研 究 報 告                
中砂(1560.TT) 
產業：電機機械 
上修資本支出用於擴產隱含展望正向，看好毛利率延續成⻑趨勢 
 投資評價與建議 
3Q26毛利率表現優於預期，且公司上修資本支出用於擴產，隱含主要業務展
望正向，加上公司積極優化產品結構隱含明年營收、獲利均具上修空間；我們
給予40倍PE，維持對公司業務之正向看法，並看好毛利率維持成⻑動能。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26三大業務營收、獲利持續改善：觀察ABU業務中，預期高階砂輪與ABF鑽
⽯機密模具需求持續成⻑，DBU業務出貨動能則維持強勁，加上公司規劃針對
SBU部分品項漲價約10%，將帶動營收、毛利率改善。我們預估本季營收有望
季增5.5%至28.4億元，毛利率44.5%，稅後EPS 4.37元。 
上修今年資本支出預算用於擴產與新品開發，隱含營運前景正向：基於DBU、
SBU客戶需求強勁，及公司持續優化產品組合，公司上修今年資本支出預算至
22.6億元(原16~17億元)，除了先前已規畫SBU 3Q27月產能至43萬片(已宣
布)，本次則上修今明年底鑽⽯碟月產能分別至8、11萬顆(原7萬顆以上/9.5萬
顆)，此外將購買土地廠房用於開發先進封裝用之減薄鑽⽯砂輪新品，以及後續
鑽⽯碟擴產需求。而在2027年展望方面，我們仍維持2027年三大業務均正向
成⻑之看法，其中DBU、SBU成⻑動能最為強勁，另ABU業務中，公司布局之
先進封裝用之減薄砂輪有望於2H27貢獻營收。預估2027年營收年增25.7%至
129.7億元，毛利率46%，稅後EPS 21.7元。 
 投資風險 
DBU擴產進度不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況 
3Q26毛利率表現優於預期：3Q26營收季增8.1%至26.9億元，毛利率42.2%，稅後EPS 3.88元，其中毛利率受惠
於：(1)台灣工具機客戶需求回溫、ABF鑽⽯機密模具用量增加，帶動ABU高階產品比重提升，挹注該業務毛利率
由2Q26的超過25%提升至3Q26的35%；(2)針對DBU大客戶之2/1.6奈米、先進封裝鑽⽯碟用量持續增加，使營收
規模擴大，我們推估毛利率由2Q26的50~55%提升至55%以上。 
4Q26三大業務營收、獲利持續改善：觀察：(1)ABU業務中，預期高階砂輪與ABF鑽⽯機密模具需求持續成⻑，至
於泰國、日本子公司出貨動能則持平看待，預估營收微幅季增，毛利率則有望持續向上；(2)DBU業務中，出貨動
能則維持強勁，因台系大客戶先進製程、封裝需求維持強勁，另美系記憶體、IDM客戶需求呈現逐季成⻑，加上
成熟製程廠商產能利用率持續提升，預估營收仍有雙位數季增；(3)SBU業務中，12吋月產能維持40萬片滿載，但
公司計畫針對部分品項價格調漲約10%，加上持續優化產品組合，將帶動營收、毛利率改善。預估本季營收有望
季增5.5%至28.4億元，毛利率44.5%，稅後EPS 4.37元。 
上修今年資本支出預算用於擴產與新品開發，隱含營運前景正向：基於DBU、SBU客戶需求強勁，及公司持續優
化產品組合，公司上修今年資本支出預算至22.6億元(原16~17億元)，除了先前已規畫SBU 3Q27月產能至43萬片
(已宣布)，本次則上修今明年底鑽⽯碟月產能分別至8、11萬顆(原7萬顆以上/9.5萬顆)，此外將購買土地廠房用於
開發先進封裝用之減薄鑽⽯砂輪新品，以及後續鑽⽯碟擴產需求。而在2027年展望方面，我們仍維持2027年三大
業務均正向成⻑之看法，動能包含：(1)預估DBU主要客戶A16/2、3奈米明年底月產能將由今年底的9~10、15~17
分別提升至14~16、19~20萬片，尤其先進製程CMP道數、規格相較於成熟製程提升，將帶動鑽⽯碟用量、平均
單價增加，另美系IDM合作順利(目前公司於18A市占約50%，公司持續推進14A之合作)，加上成熟製程、記憶體
客戶擴產挹注；(2)SBU除了有明年額外3萬片之產能挹注，公司針對現有產能也將持續透過產品組合優化等方式改
善獲利，且看好2H27美系記憶體特殊晶圓用量將比1H27成⻑；(3)ABU方面，則基於台灣ABF廠商持續擴產，公
司布局之新品先進封裝減薄砂輪有望在2H27於新廠貢獻營收，加上公司將中低毛利率產品搬至泰國、日本子公司
生產以此優化成本結構。我們預估2027年營收年增25.7%至129.7億元，毛利率46%，稅後EPS 21.7元。

### Page 3

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 November 2026 Citi Research
3
CPO: New growth opportunities from vertical integration — Hon Hai sees
significant long-term opportunities in both ASIC and vertical integration including
optical interconnect technologies. In optical networking, management believes the
transition from electrical to optical interconnects is structurally inevitable as AI
models expand and memory-bandwidth requirements increase. Hon Hai’s CPO

optical switching already starts mass volume production for its major customer,
and its micron-level precision manufacturing expertise in optical alignment,
connectors, and electrical-to-optical conversion could create additional value
through component integration and higher system-level content. Its extensive
engineering resources, ODM/JDM development capabilities, and vertically
integrated manufacturing platform should strengthen its competitive positioning.

Management expressed strong confidence in optical growth opportunities but did
not provide specific 2027 revenue guidance.
Figure 1. Hon Hai - 3Q26 Results Overview

Robust financials and earnings growth, maintain Buy — Thanks to strong
demand from major CSP clients and its continuous automation and vertical
integration development, Hon Hai’s capex increased 43% YTD. Nevertheless, its
EBITDA increased 50.3% YoY to NT$369.2bn. To improve capital efficiency, Hon

Hai plans to expand consignment arrangements, optimize inventory and
receivables management, and carefully evaluate investment payback periods.
Management reiterated its long-term operating margin floor of above 3%, with
further potential upside from scale economies, automation, product mix
improvements, and greater vertical integration.
Given Hon Hai’s solid result and better operational leverage, we raise our
2026/2027/2028 EPS by 4%/5%/5%. Considering its solid execution, stable

dividend yield and attractive valuation, we reiterate our Buy rating with TP NT$400
unchanged. Our TP implies 15x of our 2027 EPS projection.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

Hon Hai Precision
Company description
Established in 1974, Hon Hai has become the largest EMS player in the world
in terms of sales. Through its vertical integration model or "eCMMS" (e-
enabled Components, Modules, Moves and Services), it provides
components, modules and system assembly products to many global OEMs
in the PC, communication and consumer sectors.
Investment strategy
We rate Hon Hai shares as Buy as we believe the company’s margins will

continue to improve, backed by a renewed focus on profitability, operational
efficiency and new businesses development. We think there’s more upside to
go from current level, as the underlying drivers are not just about product
cycle, but also about fundamental transformation and new business
opportunities.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 15x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is
at the higher end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is

justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook,
as well as new endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon
Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe
a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 180: 261112_ml_wistron

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_ml_wistron.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Wistron

Reiterate Buy: 3Q26 beat, better margins; 
AI momentum intact; cut PO to NT$250

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 250.00 TWD | Price: 174.50 TWD

3Q26 beat: solid margins on favorable product/client mix 
Wistron’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 5.90%/4.24% came in above BofAe of 5.36%/3.66% and 
consensus of 5.55%/3.70%. We attribute the strong margins to greater sales scale 
(+28% QoQ, +102% YoY; 1%/8% above BofAe/consensus), more favorable product/client 
mix in AI business, and better results from Wiwynn, mainly supported by general/ASIC 
server momentum (see also Wiwynn 3Q26 result note). Thus, OP was 17%/24% above 
BofAe/consensus. With non-operating loss of NT$6.2bn (including net interest expense 
and FX loss), NP was 16%/27% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3).  
AI order momentum intact; ongoing project ramp-ups 
We see no change in Wistron’s AI order momentum into 4Q26 and 2027-28. We expect 
4Q26/1Q27 sales to rise 11%/8% QoQ, driven by GPU/CPU rack project ramp-ups, with 
rising ASP and spec upgrade. We also see potential sales upside from more new 
client/project wins in 2H27-2028. Although the GM decline trend is inevitable for AI 
server ODMs, we expect Wistron to post solid core OPM (i.e. excluding Wiwynn) into 
2027-28, with rising operating efficiency and more diversified client/project base. On 10 
Nov, the company announced additional capex plans of subtotal around NT$12.8bn for 
adding facilities and equipment in Taiwan, mainly to support new AI-related businesses, 
and another US$17.8mn for expanding server manufacturing capacity in Mexico.  
Trim PO to NT$250 with a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy  
We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 7-21% (but 2027-28E EPS is still 8-9% below consensus) 
to reflect the 3Q26 results beat, solid AI momentum, and sustained core OPM. Yet, we 
lower our PO to NT$250 (12x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS) from NT$265 (15x 2H26-1H27E EPS), 
with the valuation base rollover and a lower P/E multiple. We now apply 12x (1SD above 
historical average since 2022; Exhibit 8), vs. prior 15x (1.5SD above) on valuation de-
rating across AI supply chain amid macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on 
Wistron in view of its solid AI sales momentum with a broadening client/project base.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Wistron is one of the top-5 ODMs globally. Server/storage 
and PC business accounted for 73% and 24% of sales in 
2025, respectively. Key server/storage customers include 
Dell, Lenovo, HPE, Cisco, Nvidia, AMD, Intel. Key NB 
customers include Dell, Lenovo, Acer. Wistron was spun off 
from Acer (2353 TT) in 2000 and was incorporated in May 
2001. It went publicly listed on the Taiwan stock exchange 
in Aug 2003. It is headquartered in Taipei, Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to Wistron in view of improving 
sales/profit mix, solid outlook from Wiwynn, high exposure 
to server business after exiting from low-margin iPhone 
assembly business, and improving core OPM. We believe 
Wistron will benefit from Dell's strong demand in Nvidia AI 
GPU/CPU rack as the key supplier.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Wistron (WICOF) 
Our PO of NT$250 is based on 12x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS. We use 12x valuation multiple 
(around 1SD above historical P/E average since 2022) in view of high server mix, a key 
position in AI server supply chain with exposure both to component/rack business, and 
ongoing core OPM improvement.

Upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger server market demand, especially for AI, (2) 
stronger PC demand, (3) share/order win in AI server business, and (4) favorable FX 
trend.

Downside risks to our PO are (1) softer AI server demand due to AI GPU short supply or 
supply chain hiccup, (2) weaker PC market demand amid inflation/macro uncertainties, 
(3) GM pressure due to higher labor/logistics cost, (4) more intensified competition in 
NB and server business, and (5) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) 
to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating)

INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend. Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

Idea
3Q26 earnings beat on margins: Better GM of 32.5% (+1.8ppt QoQ) and well-
controlled opex of NT$3,349mn (opex ratio 12.0%) led to an operating profit of NT
$5,720mn (OPM 20.5%), 6%/11% higher than MSe/cons. at NT$5,390mn (OPM
18.9%)/NT$5,142mn (OPM 18.7%).
Power interconnects playing an increasingly important role: We estimate that
power was 65-70% of its HPC revenue in 3Q26 vs. ~50% in the past two years,
which should bring in tailwinds for its blended GM, as power interconnects in

general carry higher margins than data ones. Looking forward, we expect the
increasing power consumption in AI clusters to continue drive the growth from both
volume and spec upgrades in this space. Moreover, future power delivery
architecture could also add incremental opportunities for Bizlink.
Data interconnects diversify into optical: While the market has been concerned
about its data interconnect business recently owing to the focus on the copper

camp, we’d point out that the revenue mix from data interconnects decreased to 30-
35% of HPC in 3Q26 (or 15% of total revenue), per our estimates. Looking ahead, it
is diversifying into the optical space, including micro VCSEL based ALCs along with
its US partner, shuffle boxes/FAU alignment process for CPO and fiber patch cords.
Interplex contribution bigger than expected: Bizlink started to consolidate
Interplex from late Sep and it contributed US$67mn of revenue in Oct, or 18% of

Bizlink’s US$369mn (NT$11,846mn, +27% QoQ/+80% YoY). This is higher than the
market expectation of high single digit %. Our checks indicate that its margins are
above Bizlink’s corporate average, so the earnings might be over 20%, we estimate.
We believe this could make its offerings more comprehensive in the AI cluster and
Interplex could expand its business faster with the financial strength of Bizlink.
Stay OW: We think the growth outlook looks solid and is further strengthened by

the consolidation of Interplex. With the valuation of 11x 2027 P/E approaching the
trough valuation of 8x P/E post the recent pullback, we think risk reward looks
attractive. Stay OW with lower PT of NT$2,500 (18x 2027 P/E).

Results Summary
3Q26 earnings beat on margins
Off revenue of NT$27,875mn (+20% QoQ/+51% YoY), GM expanded by 1.8ppt to 32.5%,
vs. MSe/consensus at 32.0%/31.8%, on a more favorable revenue mix. Opex was also well
controlled at NT$3,349mn (opex ratio 12.0%) vs. MSe/consensus at NT$3,728mn (opex
ratio 13.1%)/NT$3,615mn (opex taio 13.1%), leading to an operating profit of NT$5,720mn
(OPM 20.5%), 6%/11% higher than MSe/consensus at NT$5,390mn (OPM 18.9%)/NT

$5,142mn (OPM 18.7%). With the non-op loss of NT$210mn, net profit came in at NT
$4,293mn (EPS NT$22.01), 7%/13% higher than MSe/consensus at NT$3,996mn (EPS NT
$20.67)/NT$3,789mn (EPS NT$19.27).

### Page 6

Idea
6
Valuation Methodology
Our new price target (base case scenario value) of  NT$2,500 (NT$3,665 previously) is
derived from 18x 2027e P/E (30x previously): We use price/earnings as our valuation
methodology because we believe it appropriately captures Bizlink's earnings growth
potential. We believe our target P/E multiple of 18x is justified by an earnings CAGR of
55%   over 2025-28, which implies a PEG of  0.3, lower than the average of 0.7 in 2019-24,

when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 13x for a 19% earnings CAGR. We use a
lower target multiple to factor in more conservative sentiment towards AI stocks.
Bull case scenario value – NT$4,100 (vs. prior 4,800): We apply a 30x P/E multiple (vs.
prior 40x) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes stronger AEC/ALC
demand owing to broader adoption within AI servers along with spec upgrades.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,100 (vs. prior 1,760): We apply a 8x P/E multiple (vs.

prior 15x) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes lukewarm data center
demand amid softening IT capex budgets and pricing pressure for low-barrier products in
other business segments.

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
4Q NB build forecast cut 5% despite October’s beat: October ODM NB shipments
came in 4% above MSe, but we lower our 4Q26 build forecast by 5% to 21.9M units
(-13% q/q, -32% y/y) to reflect the latest ODM guidance. The revised forecast implies

limited quarter-end pull-in strength and a deeper y/y contraction than the 26%
decline in 3Q26. Despite an already depressed 3Q base, the projected 13%
sequential decline is well below the 15-year average of flat q/q shipments in 4Q.
PC outlook seen continuing to weaken into 2027: NB build trends have weakened
over recent months, while increasingly cautious ODM commentary provides further
evidence of pressure on PC demand. Asustek expects global PC unit shipments to

decline 10–15% y/y in 2027 (link), while Quanta expects global NB shipments to
drop by high single digits to 10% y/y (link). We believe 2027 outlooks from the
supply chain have worsened in recent months, and both outlooks are directionally
consistent with our forecast for an 11.5% y/y decline in global PC units in 2027.
Looking to 1Q27, our preliminary view is a further sequential decline in NB builds,
although we have yet to establish a formal forecast.

Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' October 2026 NB shipments were 7.1M units (-27% m/m, -30% y/
y), 4% above our estimate.
We see November 2026 NB builds at 7.3M units (+4% m/m, -30% y/y).
We lower our 4Q26 NB build estimate by 5% to 21.9M units (-13% q/q, -32% y/y).
Our top five ODMs' CY26 NB build estimate is ~106.6M units (-17% y/y).

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 210: 261118_gs_WNC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261118_gs_WNC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Investment Thesis: WNC 
WNC is a leading network communications solutions provider with technology across 
WiFi, Cellular, and Satellite. The company is the leading original design manufacturer 
(ODM) for major networking brands (e.g. Cisco, HP, etc.) and serves the leading US 
telecom operators (e.g. Verizon, AT&T, etc.). WNC is also the sole supplier of Starlink’s 
low earth orbits (LEO) satellites’ user terminal (power supply, antenna and router). We 
see WNC as an early beneficiary in the growing Space Economy, riding on Starlink’s 
subscribers growth, along with recovering traditional networking business driven by a 
low base in 2025, WiFi 7, 5G FWA (fixed wireless access) investments from US and 
Europe, and customers’ needs on diversifying suppliers, leading to a 14% NI CAGR in 
2026-28E. WNC is trading at 15x 2027E P/E, lower than the average of its main peers, 
which is attractive in our view. We are Buy rated on WNC. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology: WNC 
Valuation methodology: We use a near-term P/E to derive our 12m target price for 
WNC, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our TP of NT$380.0 on 
a target P/E multiple of 28.0x on a forward year EPS (2027E). Our target P/E is derived 
from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers. 
Key downside risks: fiercer-than-expected competition in satellite communication, 
slower-than-expected Wi-Fi 7 or 5G FWA ramp-up in US and Europe markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

## Document 307: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK하이닉스 최대주주 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK하이닉스 최대주주 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
SK 하이닉스 최대주주 
SK 하이닉스 4 분기도 배당 주당 20,000 원 이상  
SK 하이닉스는 전일 주당 20,000 원(실적반영: 10,000 원, 키옥시아 특별배당: 
10,000원)의 3분기 배당을 발표하였다. 키옥시아 특별배당은 3분기 배당과 26년 회
계연도 결산배당으로 나누어 지급예정이다. 이에 SK 스퀘어는 2조 9,220억원(1분기
와 2분기 배당수입은 각 548억원)의 배당수입을 수취하게 된다. SK하이닉스는 26년 
전체 현금 배당 규모는 30조원 이상을 발표했는데, 3분기까지 지급하는 배당금은 14.6
조원이기 때문에 4분기에도 최소한 주당 20.000원 이상의 배당이 예상된다. 이 경우 
SK 스퀘어의 26 년 배당수입은 6 조원에 달할 것으로 예상한다. SK 스퀘어는 ‘26 년
~’28년 경상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 주주환원정책을 보유하고 있
어 SK스퀘어의 현 시가총액 대비 27년 주주환원율은 최소 1.5% 이상 될 전망이다.

SK 하이닉스 자사주 취득·소각으로 높아질 지배력 
SK하이닉스는 20조원의 자사주 매입을 발표하였다. 이 중 10조원은 임직원 보상목적
이며, 10조원은 주주환원 목적이다. 26년 총 60조원 이상의 자사주 취득·소각 예정(8
월 결의분 40 조원 포함)으로 임직원 보상 목적의 자사주 매입은 제외되기 때문에 26
년 실적 확정 후 추가적으로 10조원 이상의 자사주 취득이 예상된다. 기 취득한 자사주 
40조원(3.3%)는 11월 5일 소각 예정으로 소각 이후 SK스퀘어의 SK하이닉스 지분
율은 20.5%(2 분기말 기준)에서 21.2%로 상승하게 된다. 이번 발표한 10 조원
(1.0%) 자사주 소각시 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 21.4%이 되며, 추가로 10 조원을 추가 
소각한다면 21.7%까지 지분율이 상승하게 된다.

SK 하이닉스 대비 높은 베타 
SK스퀘어에 대한 투자의견 매수를 유지한다. SK스퀘어 NAV의 98%가량을 차지하
는 SK하이닉스의 현금배당 증가로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 재원이 확대되고, SK하이닉
스의 자사주 취득·소각으로 지배력이 강화되기 때문이다. SK하이닉스는 전일 컨퍼런스 
콜을 통해 29년까지 D램과 HBM 물량이 사실상 모두 솔드아웃된 상황이라 밝히며 주
가 반등 가능성이 높은데, SK 스퀘어 베타(1.8)는 SK 하이닉스(1.7) 대비 높기 때문에 
SK 하이닉스 주가 반등에 따른 SK 스퀘어의 동반 주가 상승을 기대할 수 있다. 현재 
SK스퀘어의 NAV 대비 할인율은 46.5%이며, SK하이닉스가 자사주 60조원 소각시 
할인율은 49.4%까지 상승하게 된다.

2026-10-29

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

## Document 312: An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
An RBA peak of greater conviction
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
08:56 November 13 2026 Share
We already believed the RBA cash rate was likely at a peak, even if RBA officials were less
convinced. A deeper housing downturn and early signs of cracks in the consumer sector
point to an even softer 2027. Fading inflation risks from oil also boost our conviction on
rates being at a peak.

### Page 2

It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking the cash rate after the November
increase. Since then, our conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we will publish
the November Market Outlook with revised forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing
market have all been revised down in 2027. Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for calendar 2027 more decisively inside the

RBA’s 2–3% target range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment rate forecast has
been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash
rate from here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.
A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The housing market has undershot our
forecasts over the past couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been coming in
under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan inquiries have taken another leg down in October after

the September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior months, with the additional impact
of the November rate hike still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer. Consumer income and spending have
so far been quite resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest rates. But our Westpac
Card Tracker is starting to show that discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite weak. We would not over-emphasise

this point, but it does imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB) meeting, business survey data
has shown weaker conditions and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show further
evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-labour cost shock that is not being fully
passed through to consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil and fuel prices are already materially

lower than the track assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-fourth lower than
on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of fuel costs to other prices, as well as
easing consumer inflation perceptions and expectations.
In the media conference following the November meeting, Governor Bullock flagged that it was a

good time to “take stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the market read this as a
signal that the MPB would pause at the December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to downside risks than the internal
members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is looking even less likely given signs
in the data and surveys. The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and senior

officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though, a rate hike in 2027 would require
either a material new inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would constitute a policy
error on the part of the RBA (something that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the
talents and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with the dissenting voters in the MPB

that the downside risks need more attention. Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to February, we think there will be more
evidence that the domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation outlook with it.

### Page 3

纳斯达克 100 指数期货涨 2% ，标普 500 指数期货涨 1.1% ，道指期货涨 0.7% 。现货⻩⾦上涨
0.1% ，⾄每盎司 3729.57 美元。伦敦期铜涨超 0.5% 。 Anthropic 将以每股 83 美元的价格在
纳斯达克挂牌上市，股票代码为 “ANT” 。
摘要：
Anthropic 创纪录 IPO 成为市场焦点，提振美股期货⾛⾼。与此同时，美元延续强势，铜价在前
⼀交易⽇⼤跌后有所反弹。
11 ⽉ 19 ⽇，美股期货涨幅扩⼤，纳斯达克 100 指数期货上涨 2% 。美元兑多数主要货币⾛强，彭
博美元指数有望在⼋个交易⽇内第七次上涨。周四， AI 公司 Anthropic 将以每股 83 美元的价格在
纳斯达克挂牌上市，股票代码为 “ANT” 。公司完全稀释后估值约 1.5 万亿美元，募资总额超过
1070 亿美元，创下全球 IPO 纪录。
周三，受⼈⼯智能和美国关税相关看涨押注⼤幅退潮影响，铜价暴跌近 6% ，创下 2025 年 4 ⽉以
来最⼤单⽇跌幅。不过，随着实物市场供应偏紧的局⾯持续，铜价随后出现部分反弹。

悉尼 Minotaur Capital 投资组合经理兼联合创始⼈阿⽶娜 · 罗森伯格（ Armina Rosenberg ）表
⽰： “ 短期来看，此次 IPO 是衡量投资者对⼈⼯智能兴趣的有效指标。 ”
核⼼市场⾛势如下：
募资规模突破 1070 亿美元，创纪录 IPO 提振 AI 投资信⼼
Anthropic 此次 IPO 的募资规模史⽆前例。据华尔街⻅闻⽂章，公司将 IPO 定价定为每股 83 美元，
⾼出发⾏区间上限 78 美元约 6.4% ，同时将发⾏规模从 8.25 亿股扩⼤⾄ 9.8 亿股，扩⼤约 19% 。公
司及创始⼈合计募资约 813 亿美元，叠加来⾃⾕歌、英伟达等七家科技巨头的 260 亿美元私募配
售，总募资规模突破 1070 亿美元，较 SpaceX 今年 6 ⽉的 IPO 纪录⾼出约 24% 。
悉尼 Vantage Global Prime ⾼级市场分析师 Hebe Chen 表⽰， Anthropic 上市不仅提振了市场对
整个 AI ⾏业的信⼼，也为投资者提供了新的参与渠道，使其能够通过投资前沿模型及相关应⽤，

把握 AI ⾏业的发展机遇。

### Page 2

她指出，真正的考验在于， AI 投资的整体规模能否继续扩⼤，还是投资者只是在 AI ⽣态系统的不
同领域之间重新分配资⾦。
市场抛售情绪有所缓和，⾦铜⾛⾼
现货⻩⾦上涨 0.1% ，伦敦期铜涨超 0.5% 。
德意志银⾏⾦属研究主管 Daniel Ghali 在 11 ⽉ 18 ⽇发布的报告中警告，美联储过度紧缩的担忧正
引发⾦属市场⽆差别抛售，资⾦不再区分供需基本⾯的强弱，⽽是集中撤出整个板块。铜市场
⾯临历史性逼仓、现货升⽔接近历史⾼位，⻩⾦实物需求也依然强劲，但两者价格仍⼤幅下
跌，显⽰⾦融资⾦流动和仓位调整已取代供需基本⾯，成为短期定价的主导⼒量。德银指出，
⻩⾦和铜的投机多头已⼤幅削减，但空头仍有进⼀步加仓空间，市场出清尚未结束。
德银认为，通胀预期明显回落、美联储官员却维持鹰派⽴场，是此轮抛售的核⼼诱因。尤其是
美联储理事沃勒仍为加息 50 个基点留下空间，加剧了市场对政策过度紧缩的担忧。参照 2018 年
底⾄ 2019 年初的市场动荡，德银认为，只有美联储释放转鸽信号，或股市、信贷市场出现明显

裂缝并推动⾦融条件进⼀步收紧，⾦属市场才可能迎来趋势性逆转。在此之前，尽管德银维持
⻩⾦和铜的中⻓期看涨判断，但近期价格仍⾯临显著下⾏⻛险。

3 November 2026

the Westpac-DataX Consumer

positively as well (+0.5ptps).
• Households continued to add to savings 
and buffers. Savings rose 1.3%qtr, led 
by younger households and mortgagors, 
with average savings buffers increasing 
to 22.2 months of mortgage repayments. 
• The detail shows financial pressures 
remain uneven but spending patterns 
appear to be converging across 
consumers with low, medium and high 
buffers. Much of this appears to relate 
to a reduction in fuel consumption.

Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel
The Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel is a large 
dataset that gives a timely and detailed picture 
of Australian consumer finances and behaviour. 
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics 
team, the dataset links transaction activity with 
balance sheet information to give a complete view 
of income, spending, saving and borrowing flows. 
All data is de-identified and aggregated to 
ensure privacy. The resulting sample of over 
one million customers gives an accurate 
representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer and is perfectly suited 
to quickly tracing responses to events like 
interest rate moves and energy price shocks.

Heatmap of monthly spending categories
The Westpac DataX Consumer Panel shows average
consumer spending remained resilient in Q3, rising 1.5%
despite a further lift in fuel costs. Fuel contributed 0.3ppts
to quarterly growth, but discretionary spending did more

of the heavy lifting, contributing 0.9ppts as recreation
and travel-related spending recovered. Households also
continued to add to their financial buffers: savings up 1.3%
in the quarter and average buffers across households with
a mortgage increasing to 22.2 months of repayments.
Beneath the aggregate picture, lower-buffer households
remain more exposed to higher fuel costs and mortgage
repayments, but their discretionary spending share is
looking increasingly similar to other households.

Spending holds up despite higher fuel costs
Average household spending increased 1.5% in Q3, with much
of the strength again driven by transport-related spending.
Fuel contributed 0.3ppts to quarterly growth, while transport
excluding fuel contributed a further 0.4ppts. Excluding the
direct effects of the price-driven lift in fuel, average spend
was up 1.2%qtr. Importantly, stronger fuel spending did not
come at the expense of discretionary purchases. Discretionary

categories contributed 0.9ppts to quarterly spending growth,
compared with 0.5ppts from non-fuel essentials.
Solid rise in non-fuel spend and financial buffers
Discretionary spending dominates
Recreation and culture contributed 0.3ppts to growth, reflecting
stronger spending on sporting services, while restaurants and
hotels added a further 0.2ppts. Transport spending was also
boosted by a rebound in airline expenditure, which more than
unwound the weakness recorded in Q2 when refunds relating

to cancellations due to conflict in the Middle East weighed on
the category. Discretionary spending rose 0.9%qtr, following a
flat result and a 0.1% rise in the previous two quarters.

Savings growth by mortgage status
Households continue to rebuild savings
Savings increased 1.3% in Q3, with the strongest gains again 
recorded among younger households. Savings balances for 
18–34 year olds increased 2.0%qtr, compared with 0.7% 
for those aged 55 and over. Mortgagors also continued to 
accumulate savings more rapidly, recording a 2.8% increase 
compared with 0.7% among non-mortgagors. Savings growth 
among mortgagors has now outpaced non-mortgagors for 
three consecutive years.
Stronger savings have continued to lift mortgage buffers. 
Average buffers (savings divided by the sum of essential 
expenses and minimum mortgage repayments), increased to 
22.2 months of repayments in September. Looking within the 
unit-record sample, median mortgage buffers jumped from 
6.3 months in August to 6.8 months, its highest level since the 
series began in 2019.

Median mortgage buffers
Fuel spending a clear drag on consumers
Fuel spending as a share of total spending
Spending outcomes continued to vary across age groups. 
The squeeze remains most apparent among younger 
households. Fuel spending rose 5.1%qtr for 18–34 year olds, 
against a 1.5%qtr increase in non-fuel spending. By contrast, 
non-fuel spending increased 2.1%qtr among households aged 
55 and over. 
The gap between mortgagors and non-mortgagors was 
more modest. Spending rose 1.6%qtr among mortgagors 
and 1.9%qtr among those without a mortgage, suggesting 
mortgage status alone masks considerable variation in 
household financial positions – a divide that becomes clearer 
when households are grouped by the size of their mortgage 
buffers (see discussion further on in this note). Note that ‘non 
mortgagors’ covers a diverse range of consumers including 
those that are renting and those that own their homes without 
a mortgage.

### Page 4

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Median litres of fuel consumed
More importantly, their consumption appears to be less 
responsive to price movements. During periods of cheaper 
fuel, high-buffer households show a noticeably larger lift in 
litres purchased, consistent with stockpiling when prices are 
favourable. Low-buffer households show much less of this 
response, while their fuel volumes have also adjusted less 
during the recent increase in fuel costs.
The spending data show the consequence. Fuel expenditure 
has risen sharply across all three groups recently, but remains 
highest among low-buffer households, where it also absorbs 
a larger share of total spending. Food spending has also 
declined since 2023. This may partly reflect households 
economising within the category, including switching towards 
cheaper or home-brand products, although further work is 
needed to test this.
Taken together, the households with the smallest savings 
cushions face higher mortgage repayments, spend more 
on fuel and appear to have less scope to adjust their fuel 
consumption when prices change. 
Discretionary spend as a share of total
This suggests that in aggregate, household balance sheets 
are looking healthy. The picture is a little at odds with the very 
weak reads on our Westpac-Melbourne Index Consumer 
Sentiment Index which suggests finances are coming under 
much more pressure. The fact that both average spending and 
saving both rose suggests incomes over the quarter were not 
overly stretched and that more of the weakness in sentiment 
may relate to concerns about the outlook (something that may 
also be encouraging consumers to build buffers now, while they 
still can).
Pressures intense for low-buffer households
Aggregate buffer measures mask sizeable differences across 
mortgagors. We can get a sense of these variations by 
combining mortgagors into three broad groups according to 
buffer size, i.e. low, middle and high buffer groups. This shows 
that households with the smallest buffers are also more 
exposed to both interest rates and fuel costs.

The buffer differences are relatively stark, the median savings 
of the low-buffer group just $2k compared to $15k for the 
middle-buffer group and $95k for the high buffer group. 
Low buffer households face median minimum mortgage 
repayments of around $2,600 a month, compared with $2,400 
for the middle buffer group and $2,200 for the highest-buffer 
group. 
Low buffer households also spend more on fuel, at around 
$170 a month compared with $160 for the middle buffer 
group and $150 for the high buffer group.

We can use average pump prices to estimate what this 
expenditure implies for fuel volumes. Low-buffer households 
consume around 70 litres a month, middle-buffer households 
67 litres a month and the high-buffer group around 62 litres. 
This compares to an average of 81, 80 and 70 respectively 
through 2024-25. 
Median fuel spend by customer

### Page 5

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Yet despite this greater exposure, the gap in broader spending 
behaviour has narrowed. High-buffer households continue 
to spend more on discretionary categories, averaging around 
$1,940 a month compared with $1,740 for low-buffer 
households. However, their discretionary share has been 
broadly stable since 2023, while the shares of low- and 
middle-buffer households have risen by close to a percentage 
point bringing them more in line with the high-buffer group.

Some of the convergence reflects a longer run down-trend in 
fuel spending that has been more pronounced amongst low 
and middle buffer households. This lowering in fuel spend lifts 
the share available for other categories.

Essential spending excluding fuel, meanwhile, has changed 
little across the three cohorts since 2023. The shift in 
spending patterns has largely occurred on the discretionary 
side.

The result is a somewhat mixed picture. Low-buffer 
households remain considerably more exposed to mortgage 
and fuel costs and have far smaller savings cushions. But their 
discretionary spending has nevertheless moved closer to that 
of better-buffered households, suggesting some recovery in 
spending flexibility despite these constraints. 
Conclusion
Aggregate household finances remain resilient: spending and 
savings both increased in Q3 and mortgage buffers continued 
to rebuild. Discretionary spending has also held up despite the 
sharp increase in fuel costs.

But aggregate resilience masks a much more uneven 
distribution of pressure. Low-buffer households 
simultaneously face higher mortgage repayments, greater fuel 
exposure and much smaller savings cushions. While these 
households have so far maintained discretionary spending, 
their capacity to absorb further pressure is clearly more 
limited.
About the Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics service for 
institutional and business banking customers, the dataset links 
transaction activity with balance sheet information to give 
a complete view of income, spending, saving and borrowing 
flows. 
The resulting sample of over one million customers provides 
an accurate representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer. Monthly observations are available back 
to January 2019 with timely updates provided a few weeks 
after the end of each month. As such, it is perfectly suited to 
quickly tracing the impact and responses to events like interest 
rate moves and energy price shocks.

## Document 379: Germany’s hidden advantage in the AI race

Source PDF: all report pdf/Germany’s hidden advantage in the AI race.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Berlin may be Germany’s innovation powerhouse, but across
Europe, the country is still playing catch-up. When it comes to
AI, however, it could have much more up its sleeve than its
overall innovation performance suggests

Franziska Biehl and Carsten Brzeski
Once we turn from traditional innovation to artiﬁcial intelligence, Germany
suddenly looks much more competitive
Germany’s innovation problem runs deep…
Germany’s innovation landscape remains highly fragmented. Berlin
tops our national Innovation Index for the 12th consecutive year,
followed by Hamburg and Baden-Württemberg, while Thuringia,
Mecklenburg-Western Pomerania and Saxony-Anhalt remain at the
bottom, held back by demographic pressures, skills shortages and
weak business creation.
These regional diﬀerences reﬂect the structural challenges holding
back innovation capacity across the country. Consequently,
Germany only ranks 16th out of the 21 eurozone countries in our
ING Germany Innovation Index for the eurozone, leaving one of

### Page 2

Europe’s largest economies in the lower half of the table. Ireland
remains the eurozone’s innovation leader, followed by Luxembourg
and Estonia, while Bulgaria, Greece and Italy sit at the bottom.
At ﬁrst sight, Germany's low position in our innovation ranking isn't
good news for its economic prospects. However, in the age of AI,
inventing new technologies is increasingly becoming a smaller part
of the equation. The winners might be those able to adopt,

integrate and put AI applications to productive use quickly. And
once we turn from traditional innovation to artiﬁcial intelligence,
Germany suddenly looks much more competitive.
…but on AI, it jumps ahead
Our new ING Germany AI Index, covering research, investment,
patents, adoption and ICT skills, puts Germany in sixth place,
suggesting the country already has many of the ingredients
needed to turn AI’s potential into economic gains.
The countries at the top of our ranking have a clear advantage

when it comes to research. Finland recorded more than six high-
impact AI publications per capita, while Luxembourg produced
more than 10. Germany managed only 3.5. The gap in funding is
even larger: relative to GDP, Finland invested around ﬁve times as
much in AI start-ups as Germany.
But digital talent could matter just as much. The AI race isn't only
about developing models; it's also about having enough people
who know what to do with them.
Digital talent and AI adoption

### Page 3

Source: Eurostat
In Germany, more than 5% of employees work in ICT-related jobs
and roughly 6% of university graduates complete their studies in
an ICT-related ﬁeld. Around one in four German businesses already
uses AI, and Germany is among the leading locations for AI-related
patents.
The same pattern emerges elsewhere in Europe. In Luxembourg,
Finland and the Netherlands, between 7% and 9% of employees
work in ICT-related jobs. At the same time, these countries are

among Europe’s frontrunners in AI adoption, with around one in
three businesses and one in four individuals already using AI.
Greece sits at the other end of the spectrum: its pool of ICT
specialists is much smaller, and fewer than one in ten businesses
use AI.
Digital talent could be Germany’s AI edge
Germany's comparative strength may lie less in developing its own
large AI models and more in applying them across its industrial
base and providing the infrastructure to do so. Investment plans are

moving in that direction too. The German government plans to at
least double data centre capacity by 2030 and quadruple available
AI computing capacity. International technology companies,
including Nvidia and Amazon, are also investing in German AI
computing and cloud infrastructure.
Building innovative capacity takes years, if not decades. But
advantages from adopting existing technology can emerge much

及歐股，因市場擔憂民主黨全面掌控國會恐

求民 主 黨 的跨 黨 合 作，仍 無 法緩 解 市 場擔
憂，美股主要指數普遍收跌；歐股方面，面
對全球科技股評價修正，部分資金轉入歐洲

連結而同步下跌，港股則是受惠騰訊發布新

熱門議題
► 黃金價格歷經第二季修整，近日呈現大幅反
彈走勢，主要係因美國期中選舉後，市場憂
心AI產業監管將趨嚴，加上晶片與科技股遭
遇大幅修正，促使部分資金轉往黃金等防禦
型資產尋求避險。
► 根據世界黃金協會最新報告，第3季全球央行
淨買進321公噸黃金，較第二季明顯回升，年
增率達42%，創下自2024年第四季以來的單
季新高，前3季累計買進金額更是高達675公
噸，顯示黃金儲備已經列入全球央行必需資
產配置而且比重維持穩定增長態勢。
► 長期以來黃金具有抗通膨特性，觀察黃金與
聯邦基金利率走勢，從2020年之後，即便全
球歷經2次升息循環，黃金價格仍呈現穩健上
揚走勢，反應其抗通膨保值特性。此外，美
國財政赤字壓力難消，恐令美元長期購買力
被稀釋， 加 速 全 球 央 行 去 美 元 化 ， 有 鑑 於
此，建議黃金納入資產配置。

分裂政府中東僵局，資金輪動黃金避險

► 醫療類股於美國第三季財報展現強勁的獲利
韌性，實際獲利優於預期程度為主要產業中
表現最佳，背後主要受惠大型製藥企業(如：
禮來、再生元製藥、吉利德科學)在減重藥、
癌症治療及創新藥領域之需求持續增長，帶
動整體產業獲利表現優於市場預期。
► 期中選舉後醫療政策輪廓逐漸明朗。民主黨
主張支持擴大醫療保險覆蓋、延續平價醫療
法案補貼以及緩和醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)
削減，有助提升醫療服務使用率；雖藥價談
判政策恐帶來部分定價壓力，但整體政策主
張仍對創新藥、生技研發及醫療保險產業具
潛在政策支持效果。
► 綜上，在AI投資回報率面臨市場檢驗之際，
市場資金持續尋求防禦性配置。醫療保健類
股與資訊科技類股相關性偏低，有助分散投
資組合對單一AI題材依賴，加上第三季獲利
動能轉強、創新藥需求穩健及產業能見度提
升，後續有望持續受惠資金輪動效應。

財報與政策題材雙利多，醫療類股成市場新寵兒

政策議題 民主黨傾向 共和黨傾向 兩黨共識對製藥產業潛在影響
降低藥價 依《通膨削減法案》框架，擴大醫療 補助計劃(Medicaid)藥價談判適用範圍 傾向透過最惠國定價機制來降低藥 價 兩黨均主張降低藥價 製藥企業面臨定價壓力
美國本土製造 透過研發補助、稅收優惠及產業政策 鼓勵藥廠回流美國生產 透過關稅、供應鏈重組及企業承諾 推動製藥產能回流 兩黨均支持醫藥供應鏈自主化 有利本土藥廠及原料藥供應鏈投資
醫療保險覆蓋
支持延長《平價醫療法案》補貼、擴
大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)覆蓋範圍，
並緩和該補助計劃的削減
傾向由州政府及市場機制主導醫療
體系，限制聯邦政府支出
醫療的可負擔性為兩黨政策共同焦點，有助
增加就醫人口及處方藥使用量，支撐製藥、
醫療服務及保險業長期需求
FDA審查資源 支持增加FDA審查預算與人力配置 著重監管效率優化，額外資源投入 相對有限 兩黨均支持提升新藥審查效率 利多創新藥與生技公司

投資焦點必需消費
► 短期而言，檢視各類股在最近股災表現，美
國成長股、資訊科技、與通訊服務等高波動
度類股，表現明顯落後大盤，其中，資訊科
技與通訊服務波動度最大、跌幅最重，主要
係因美股科技巨頭財報不如預期、負自由現
金流量，疊加期中選舉後民主黨恐緊縮AI基
礎建設進程及資本支出見頂等不確定因素，
令市場 擔憂 導致 資 金流出 ；並轉 進核 心消
費、健康護理、防禦股、公用事業等防禦性
產業，報酬相對較穩健，受市場資金青睞。
► 長期而言，從2008迄今，核心消費類股長期
表現穩健上漲。與主要指數相比，面對近月
AI科技股修正，同期間費半大跌18%、那斯
達克跌幅6%、標普跌幅1%，而核心消費類
股大漲13%，反應其低波動、抗震韌性。
► 整體而言，從2015年至今，歷經2次通膨與
升息循環，核心消費受影響性低，有鑑於資
產配置角度，建議短期跟隨資金流向配置核
心消費股，提高投組抗震韌性。
必需消費長期表現穩健、抗通膨、低波動避險資金流入，必需消費類股單月表現最佳

配置必需消費類股，提高投組抗震韌性

股市
 隨著美國期中選舉結束，美伊衝突可望降溫，市場關注焦點將回歸基本面，惟AI資料中心建設進程
或將受到州政府緊縮政策而造成評價面壓力增添股價波動。短期可布局防禦股、分散配置於不同國
家與產業，增強抗震力、降低市場波動影響，長期而言，AI從全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高
的品質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨勢不變。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高的品
質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨
勢不變，非AI部分可關注必需消費產業、
醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 隨資金湧入，公債殖利率回落，惟根據最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升
息空間，立場偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走升
時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引

力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 聯準會12月份會議升息態度偏鷹，美元高檔整理。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理，日圓弱勢盤整
商品
 美國期中選舉結束，預期中東地緣衝突降溫，油價從近期高點回落，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 473: What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it_ (2026-10-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it_ (2026-10-11).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-10-11
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it?
A closer look at the impact of China’s currency on China’s export growth.
Sean Yong/Reuters
UPDATED
October 11, 2026 12:00 a.m.
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
There is an argument—made in the recent Geneva
Report and by various former IMF officials—that
China’s current export surge is the simple
consequence of the housing downturn and the
resulting weakness in Chinese internal demand.*
The weakness in the RMB in this story is entirely
incidental to the increase in exports, which is, more or
less, a mechanical response to weak internal demand.

Cannot sell excavators at home? Export them.
Built more modern car capacity than your internal
market supports? Export that too…
The fall in the nominal RMB over the course of 2022
and the first half of 2023 in nominal and read terms
was simply a function of domestic macroeconomic
weakness, and a growing rate differential. It wasn’t an
active policy choice by China’s central bank.

### Page 3

3
Now, some might note that China’s currency doesn’t
always move with rate differentials. The RMB didn’t
consistently get pulled up when Chinese rates were
higher than U.S. rates (2002-2020 generally
speaking), and while it did fall when rate differentials
moved against the RMB in 2022 and 2023, it then
stabilized and has even appreciated modestly since
the middle of last year. That appreciation hasn’t been
big enough to correct the deep undervaluation of the

RMB, but it came at a time when the PBOC policy
rate (the reverse repo rate) was well below the U.S.
policy rate.
China doesn’t have an open financial account. As a
result, equilibrium in the foreign exchange market is
thus driven far more by exporter conversion and
current account flows than by rate differentials and
financial flows.** That, together with China’s ongoing
management of the exchange rate and a trade surplus
that can generate significant inflows, means that the

### Page 4

4
value of the RMB is much more of a policy variable
under the independent control of the Chinese
authorities than a simple byproduct of monetary and
fiscal policies.
The modern IMF is reluctant to accept this
conclusion. Its surveillance has generally ignored
China’s currency management (even if that
management is relatively obvious; see the data on FX
settlement) and its recent comprehensive assessment
of China’s economy completely ignored the balance of

payments and exchange rate management.
But it is pretty obvious to the market, even if the
PBOC, rather absurdly, insists that “market forces
play a decisive role in determining the RMB
exchange rate.”
I doubt the PBOC can win this argument. I agree
with Mark Sobel:
“The renminbi is still heavily managed and the
authorities have acted to slow appreciation. The paper
states that the PBoC phased out ‘regular’ foreign
exchange intervention in 2017. But it has many

means at its disposal to achieve the same effect
through extensive capital controls, administrative
guidance, signalling at the fix and deploying state-
owned commercial banks. These means are heavily
used. Thus, the word ‘regular’ must be taken with a
pinch of salt. The comparison to Japan, which

### Page 5

5
intervenes occasionally, is spurious – the yen largely
floats freely in integrated global markets. The same
cannot be said for the renminbi.”
In reality, the PBOC’s daily fix now plays the decisive
role in the currency’s path, and the market is often a
bet on how the PBOC’s management of the yuan will
evolve.
That is clear from the lack of volatility in the yuan’s
rate against the dollar. The yuan’s move down against
the dollar in 2022 was a controlled 10 percent move,

and the appreciation over the last 16 months has been
even more controlled crawl.
And the market is clearly massaged with substantial
help from the state banks. In fact that the pattern of
changes in the monthly FX settlement data looks
exactly like the pattern of intervention of a central
bank managing its currency around a tight band (the

### Page 6

6
state banks in practice buy FX to keep the CNY from
testing the strong side of the band, so there is an
asymmetry in how the CNY is managed)
None of this would matter much if China’s economy
was small. But that obviously isn’t the case. Nor would
this matter much if China’s exports didn’t respond to
changes in the RMB’s real (and nominal) value. But
that is also not the case.
There is abundant evidence that China’s exports do
respond to moves in the currency. Periods of RMB

weakness have consistently been associated with
periods of especially rapid Chinese export growth.

### Page 7

7
Chancellor Merz and President Macron got this
right, even if macroeconomic issues haven’t yet
figured prominently in the EU’s dialogue with
China.*** As they note in their open letter to the
European Commission:
“An appreciation of undervalued currencies towards a value
more in line with fundamentals would help support the
rebalancing of trade accounts with the EU in the short and
medium term … European policy discussions focused on
forging a common understanding of these exchange rate issues

and their interaction with global imbalances would be useful.”
The best evidence that exports responded to changes
in the currency, and not simply to changes in the path
of China’s internal demand, is the acceleration in
China’s export growth after the RMB’s depreciation
over the course of 2022.
This is the killer chart.

### Page 8

8
Now, you might ask: what of the acceleration in
export growth in 2020 and 2021? That was pretty
clearly tied to the global increase in goods demand
that came with the pandemic and work from home
requirements. It was expected to fade when the world
economy normalized. But it didn’t. By 2023 Chinese
export growth again topped global trade growth.
The general expectation that the rise in China’s goods
surplus during Covid would prove to be “transitory”

### Page 9

9
heavily influenced the IMF’s work at the time: see the
IMF’s external sector reports of 2023 and 2024.
I would argue that neglecting the exchange rate was
the key reason the IMF’s kept forecasting, incorrectly,
that China’s current account surplus would fall (See
this blog with Shahin Vallee). The IMF certainly
didn’t warn that China’s property downturn and the
RMB’s real depreciation risked a second China shock
in its 2022, 2023, or 2024 surveillance reports.

Concretely, the weaker RMB appears to have
contributed to a very large outperformance of China’s
exports relative to global trade. See the following
chart
That incidentally also happened after the RMB
followed the dollar down in 2003.
In both periods, there were significant structural
shifts. The WTO accession in 2002, and the success

### Page 10

10
of Chinese industrial policies in the “green” industrial
sectors and autos in 2022. In both cses exports were
turbocharged when structural shifts that should have
resulted in a stronger currency were instead amplified
by currency weakness.
No matter. Paul Krugman has noted that a country
that is converging technologically with its trade
partners should see a trend appreciation in its
currency. China got the technological convergence,
but not the real appreciation—at least not until this

year. And the move since the middle of 2025 has
corrected the depreciation of the RMB that occurred
when the RMB followed the dollar down on
liberation day, but not the structural overvaluation.
Now, there is an argument that exchange rate
adjustment is too hot an issue to make it a priority.
The PBOC isn’t interested it seems.
And some believe that in the interest of comity and
good relations with China, the IMF should mirror
the PBOC’s views. After all a stronger RMB would

slow China’s exports and thus slow China’s economy
at a time when China needs export growth to escape
deflation and generate employment. Or so the
argument goes. Concerns about “external balance“
and the negative global spillovers from China’s rising
goods surplus should be ignored and the Fund’s sole
focus should be on policies that lead to internal

### Page 11

11
balance. That provides one rationale for the Fund’s
ongoing decision to essentially ignore the contentious
issue of the PBOC’s ongoing exchange rate
management.
That argument, however, assumes that China’s
economy is small and thus China’s external surplus
can safely be ignored because it isn’t a drag on the
growth of the IMF’s other members.
That clearly isn’t true.
And it isn’t clear that China is more interested in
having a negotiation over its level of basic pension

benefits or the size of its fiscal deficit (where the
IMF continues to make timid recommendations that
would not move the needle—and certainly wouldn’t
meaningfully reduce China’s external surplus) than a
discussion over its currency practices.****
Back in the world of fixed but adjustable exchange
rates, there was an expectation (not always met to be
sure) that surplus countries would allow their
currencies to strengthen to bring their economies

back into external balance, and that other policy tools
would be used to ensure internal balance.
I would argue that China’s exchange rate (against
both the dollar and the basket) has many similarities
to the old world of fixed but adjustable currencies.
True, it moves on a controlled path when it moves

### Page 12

12
between different levels rather than jumping up and
down. But the PBOC does frequently target a level,
and that targeted level does change over time.
I would further argue that steps like the recent tariff
rate quota that will limit China’s exports of hybrid
cars to Europe to around 350,000 units next year (up
from 2025, but well below the 800,000 units of
2026) aren’t going to address the underlying
imbalance on their own. They can provide a bit of a

buffer for specific sectors.
But that buffer, while necessary, won’t address the
broad-based erosion of manufacturing capacity
outside China. This erosion will persist as long as an
undervalued RMB sends the entire world a signal to
use China as its production base.
The new system of managed trade that is emerging in
autos would need to be progressively expanded to an
ever-increasing set of sectors if protection is used as a
substitute for both domestic reform in China and

exchange rate adjustment.
That is now the direction of travel, but it doesn’t
address the underlying systemic issues.
Incidentally, those who believe the path of internal
demand is critical to rebalancing and that exchange
rate moves are secondary need to explain how an
acceleration in domestic demand would, on its own,

### Page 13

13
lead China’s external accounts to adjust. One
important, stylized fact about China is that domestic
demand growth is no longer feeding over to import
growth (ex-gold, that is).
Bottom line: China, which needs a 1.5 percentage
point contribution from net exports every year, is on a
collision course with its trading partners without a
currency correction that provides the basis for more
balanced trade even in the absence of trade
restrictions.
And there isn’t a realistic path towards balance that

doesn’t involve a stronger RMB; there aren’t, as Paul
Krugman once put it, immaculate transfers.
* That downturn shows up in domestic deflationary
pressure, and weak job growth/high rates of (at least
youth) unemployment. China, in other words, isn’t in
“internal balance.” I really liked the report from

### Page 14

14
Kamakshya Trivedi and Hui Shan of Goldman Sachs
arguing China needs two tools to hit two targets
(internal and external balance).
** To be sure, rate differentials matter for exporter
conversion—but so does the expected movement of
the RMB. The market understands this better than
the Fund. The Chen and Suwanapruti (Goldman
Sachs) note uses a framework similar to mine to
describe the CNY’s moves in Q3:
“In our view, sizeable FX inflows have continued to support

RMB appreciation despite unfavorable interest rate
differentials, as strong export receipts and increased exporter
FX conversions have outweighed portfolio outflows. Our
estimated FX conversion ratio rose to 63% in August,
compared with around 45% in normal years, likely reflecting a
shift in market expectations from RMB depreciation to
appreciation.”
*** Š ef č ovi č  has jurisdiction over trade policy
(narrowly defined), but not broader macroeconomic

policies. Europe’s institutional architecture for
exchange rate coordination is not well-developed.
**** The current account surplus is no longer masked
to the same extent as in 2023 and 2024, as SAFE has
fiddled with its BoP methodology once again (per the
Federal Reserve) and the BoP goods surplus is no
longer significantly below the customs goods surplus.
Of course, there are still problems in the data—the
deficit in the investment income account most

## Document 528: インフレ警戒も利上げを急がないＥＣＢ ～長期金利上昇が追加利上げ判断を複雑に～

Source PDF: all report pdf/インフレ警戒も利上げを急がないＥＣＢ ～長期金利上昇が追加利上げ判断を複雑に～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Europe Trends

2026 年10 月 30 日

1/5 
インフレ警戒も利上げを急がないＥＣＢ 
～長期金利上昇が追加利上げ判断を複雑に～ 
 経済調査部 首席エコノミスト  田中 理 
（℡：050-5474-7494）

（要旨） 
○ ＥＣＢは 10 月 29 日の理事会で、政策金利の据え置きを全会一致で決定した。６月と９月の
利上げ後もエネルギー価格の上昇が続き、物価の上振れリスクが高まっているが、基調的な
インフレが概ね安定し、賃金への二次的波及も限定的なことから、追加利上げを見送った。
前回９月の理事会以降、国債利回りの上昇などで金融環境が引き締まっていることも据え置
き判断に影響した。 
○ 今回の理事会では利上げの是非が議論され、ラガルド総裁は今後も追加利上げを選択肢に残
すことを示唆している。フランスの財政不安による国債利回りの上昇は、金融政策の波及を
妨げるリスクを伴う一方、景気や物価を抑制する効果もある。12 月理事会では、新たな経済
見通しに加え、エネルギー価格の持続性、企業の価格転嫁、賃金動向、金融環境の引き締ま
りが判断材料となる。筆者は 12 月、来年３月、６月の追加利上げで政策金利が 3.25％に達
するとの従来予想を維持するが、利上げの時期や回数を巡る不確実性が高まっている。

■全会一致で据え置きも利上げを議論 
欧州中央銀行（ＥＣＢ）は 10 月 29 日に終わった理事会で、政策金利の据え置きを全会一致で
決定した。ＥＣＢは６月に利上げを再開し、７月に据え置いた後、９月に再び利上げしたが、今
回は追加利上げが見送られた。インフレ懸念が後退したわけではない。９月のユーロ圏の消費者
物価上昇率は前年比＋3.8％と８月の同＋3.2％から加速し、２％の中期的な物価安定を大きく上
回っている。中東情勢の緊迫が長期化するなか、原油や天然ガス価格は９月に発表したＥＣＢの
スタッフ見通しでの想定を大きく上回っており、物価の上振れリスクが高まっている。 
理事会後の記者会見でラガルド総裁は、今回の理事会で利上げが適切かどうかを議論したこと
を明らかにした。それでも今回の利上げを見送ったのは、①物価上昇が今のところエネルギー価
格が中心で、基調的なインフレ率が概ね安定していること、②賃金や他の物価への二次的波及が
限定的なこと、③前回理事会以降の長期金利上昇などで金融環境が引き締まっていること、④エ
ネルギー高が景気を下押しする影響も見極める必要があること―などが背景にある。

### Page 2

2/5 
■金融環境の引き締まりを強調 
 ９月と10 月の声明文を比較すると、物価見通しに対する上振れリスクと景気見通しに対する下
振れリスクを警戒する基本姿勢は変わっていない。ただし、エネルギーショックの規模と持続期
間が拡大し、他の物価への波及が強まる可能性をより明確に指摘している。また、前回理事会以
降に金融環境が引き締まっており、今後の物価と成長を押し下げるとの認識を新たに示した。 
 ９月の声明文では、中東での紛争によってインフレ圧力が継続し、物価上昇率が長期にわたり
目標を上回ると説明していた。これに対して、10 月の声明文では、エネルギー価格がさらに上昇
していることを確認したうえで、エネルギーショックの規模と持続期間が拡大し、間接的・二次
的波及を通じてインフレ率を押し上げ、成長率を押し下げる可能性を指摘した。同時に、基調的
なインフレが概ね安定し、賃金が目立って反応していないことを改めて強調した。物価の上振れ
リスクが高まっているものの、利上げを急ぐ状況には至っていないとの判断が読み取れる。 
 注目されるのが金融環境に関する認識の変化である。９月時点でも市場金利の上昇は確認され
ていたが、10 月の声明文では、前回理事会以降の金融環境の引き締まりが物価と成長を押し下げ
る可能性を政策判断の冒頭で明示している。金利上昇で資金調達コストが上がれば、設備投資や
住宅投資、個人消費が抑制される。ＥＣＢが政策金利を追加的に引き上げなくても、金融環境が
引き締まっていることを意味する。

■財政不安や金融市場の動揺が新たなリスク 
 ＥＣＢはユーロ圏経済について、７～９月期も成長が続いたとの認識を示した。中東紛争の開
始後に落ち込んだサービス業は持ち直し、製造業、デジタルサービス、輸出は人工知能（ＡＩ）
関連の設備投資に支えられている。国防やインフラ関連の政府支出も景気を下支えしている。 
一方、足元のエネルギー価格の再上昇が消費者心理を悪化させており、資金調達コストの上昇
も景気の重荷となりつつある。８月の失業率は低水準を維持したものの、雇用者数や労働力人口
の増加ペースは鈍化している。短期的な景気拡大が続くとしながらも、中東情勢の混乱とエネル
ギー価格の更なる上昇や高止まり、世界的な金融市場の動揺、貿易摩擦の再燃などを背景に、景
気見通しに対するリスクが下振れ方向にあるとの判断を維持した。 
 ９月からの変化として重要なのは、財政の不透明感が金融環境を引き締め、家計や企業の信頼
感を損なうリスクを新たに明確化したことである。声明では特定国を名指ししていないものの、
フランスの財政再建の難航と国債利回りの急上昇が念頭にあるものと考えられる。また、国債市
場の混乱が他国に波及することで金融環境が引き締まり、需要が減少する危険性にも言及した。
エネルギー高が物価を押し上げる一方、長期金利上昇や金融環境の悪化が景気を抑制するとい
う、政策判断の難しい状況が続いている。

■賃金への二次的波及は限定的 
 今回の記者会見で注目されたのが、エネルギー価格の上昇が他の物価や賃金にどの程度波及し
ているかに関する説明である。ＥＣＢは、川上段階ではエネルギー高による物価の押し上げ圧力

### Page 3

3/5 
が表れ始めていると認識している。企業がエネルギー価格の上昇分を製品やサービスの販売価格
に転嫁する間接的な影響は次第に強まっている。ただし、雇用者報酬の伸びや、妥結賃金を集計
したＥＣＢの賃金トラッカーは、今のところ落ち着いている。また、短期の期待インフレ率は上
昇しているものの、長期の期待インフレ率の多くは２％程度で安定している。現時点ではエネル
ギー高が賃金と物価の持続的な上昇を招く悪循環には陥っていない。 
 もっとも、エネルギー価格の高止まりが長期化すれば、企業の価格転嫁が広がり、生活費の上
昇を補う賃上げ要求も強まりやすい。こうした間接的・二次的波及が長期化し、２％目標への物
価の回帰を妨げることを警戒している。

■長期金利上昇で金融環境が引き締まり 
金融環境に関するＥＣＢの認識にも変化がみられる。９月の利上げ後、ユーロ圏の長期金利は
さらに上昇した。背景には、世界的な国債需給の悪化、米国の長期金利上昇、ＥＣＢの保有国債
縮小、各国の財政不安などがある。ラガルド総裁は記者会見で、前回理事会以降に長期の資金調
達コストが明らかに上昇したことを認めた。銀行貸出にも引き締めの影響が表れている。ＥＣＢ
が 10 月27 日に発表した７～９月期の銀行貸出調査によれば、企業向け融資と消費者信用の貸出
基準が一段と厳格化し、融資需要も幅広い分野で減少した。 
追加利上げを判断する際には、こうした金融環境の引き締まりを考慮する必要がある。政策金
利を引き上げなくても、長期金利の上昇や銀行融資の厳格化によって実体経済への引き締め効果
が強まっている。もっとも、成長期待の改善に伴う金利上昇と、財政不安やリスクプレミアムの
拡大による金利上昇では、景気や物価への影響も異なる。ＥＣＢは市場金利の動向だけでなく、
企業や家計の借入金利、銀行の貸出姿勢、金融政策の波及状況を総合的に判断する必要がある。

■フランスの財政不安と利上げ継続の整合性 
 長期金利上昇のなかでも特に注目されるのが、フランスの財政不安である。フランスの10 年国
債利回りは 10 月下旬に５％を超え、ドイツ国債との利回り格差も大幅に拡大した。政府の財政再
建策に対する国民の反発や、議会での予算審議の難航が市場の不安を強めている。今回の声明文
ではフランスを名指ししていないものの、財政の持続可能性が広範な経済安定の重要な支えであ
ると強調した。また、財政の不透明感が金融環境を一段と引き締め、家計や企業の信頼感を損な
う可能性を指摘した。 
 ラガルド総裁は記者会見で、政府債務の大きい国々は、以前よりも高い金利が今後も続く可能
性を財政計画に織り込む必要があると説明し、各国の財政運営がＥＵの財政枠組みに適合するこ
との重要性を強調した。そのうえで、市場の動きが金融政策の適切な波及を脅かす場合、伝達保
護手段（ＴＰＩ）を利用する可能性があることを示唆している。ＴＰＩは各国間の国債利回り格
差の不当で無秩序な拡大に対応するため、2022 年７月に創設された。ＴＰＩは特定国の財政負担
を軽減するための制度ではなく、金融政策の適切な波及を確保するためのものである。財政の持
続可能性などに関する条件もあり、フランス国債の利回りが上昇したからといって自動的に発動

### Page 4

4/5 
されるわけではない。 
フランスの国債市場が一段と混乱した場合、ＥＣＢが量的引き締め（ＱＴ）のペースを緩める
との見方も一部にある。過去の資産買い入れプログラムで保有する国債の償還元本の再投資を再
開し、必要に応じて国別配分を柔軟化することで、市場の安定化を図るとの考え方だ。ただし、
10 月理事会ではＡＰＰ・ＰＥＰＰの再投資を行わず、保有資産の縮小を続ける従来の方針が維持
され、ラガルド総裁も記者会見でＱＴの減速や停止について具体的な方針を示さなかった。ＱＴ
の見直しを正当化するには、保有資産の縮小自体が市場の混乱や金融政策の伝達障害を引き起こ
していることを見極める必要がある。 
フランスの財政不安があるからといって、ＥＣＢが追加利上げを断念するとは限らない。中期
的な物価安定の確保には追加利上げが必要となる一方、特定国で不当で無秩序な市場の混乱が生
じた場合には、金融政策の伝達を守るための手段を別途活用するという政策の組み合わせは可能
である。もっとも、国債売りの背景に財政運営への正当な懸念がある場合、ＴＰＩの発動には慎
重な判断が求められる。フランスの財政再建が進まない場合、利上げを進めながら国債市場の安
定を確保することは、ＥＣＢにとって難しい課題となる。

■12 月に改めて利上げの是非を判断 
ラガルド総裁は理事会後の記者会見で、利上げの是非を実際に議論したことを明らかにした。
また、12 月の次回会合までの間に多くの経済データが発表され、金融政策を判断するうえで重要
な情報が得られると説明した。こうした発言からは、エネルギー価格の上昇が続き、物価の上振
れリスクが強まっている以上、追加利上げが引き続き有力な選択肢となることが示唆される。 
 ただし、先行きの金融政策については、特定の金利経路を予め約束せず、新たに入手可能とな
ったデータに基づき、理事会毎に政策金利を判断する従来からの方針を維持した。また、９月時
点で預金ファシリティ金利は 2.50％に達し、ＥＣＢが様々なモデルで推計する中立金利のレンジ
上限付近にある。追加利上げは、金融政策をより明確な引き締め領域に移すことになるため、中
期的な物価安定の確保に追加の金融引き締めが必要かを十分に検証することが必要となる。 
今回の記者会見で、ラガルド総裁は追加利上げの方向性を示唆する一方、時期やペースを約束
することは避けた。今後の利上げ判断は、エネルギー高による物価上昇圧力と金融環境の引き締
まりによる需要抑制効果を総合的に判断することになる。次回12 月の理事会は、四半期に１回の
スタッフ見通しの発表月で、経済・物価を取り巻く環境をより詳細に検討することになる。

■ラガルド総裁が早期退任 
今回の理事会の結果発表に先立ち、ＥＣＢはラガルド総裁が 2027 年２月７日付で早期退任する
ことを正式に発表した。本来の任期満了である2027 年 10 月末より約９ヶ月早い退任となる。後
任総裁はＥＵ条約で定められた手続きに基づき、欧州理事会によって任命される。早期退任の理
由について、ラガルド総裁は記者会見で、金融政策以外の問題について自由に発言し、活動した
いとの意向を示した。ＥＣＢの幹部には独立性と引き換えに政治的中立性を守る義務があり、発

### Page 5

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
言や活動にも制約がある。ただし、退任後の具体的な活動や就任予定の役職を正式に明らかにし
たわけではない。今回の早期退任により、2027 年春のフランス大統領選挙を前に、ＥＵの各国首
脳が後継総裁を選ぶことができる。フランスの政治情勢がＥＣＢ人事に影響することを避けやす
くなるという側面があるが、本人がこれを退任理由として説明したわけではない。 
後任候補としては、オランダ中銀前総裁のクノット氏、スペイン中銀前総裁で国際決済銀行
（ＢＩＳ）の総支配人を務めるデコス氏などの名前が取り沙汰されている。2027 年にはラガルド
総裁に加え、シュナーベル理事、レーン理事も退任する。次期執行部の構成によっては、中長期
的な金融政策の姿勢や市場との対話のあり方に変化が生じる可能性がある。

■利上げ継続を予想も、金融環境の引き締まりに注意 
 12 月16・17 日の次回理事会では、新たなスタッフ見通しが発表される。９月の見通しは８月
19 日までの市場前提に基づいており、その後の原油・天然ガス価格は想定を上回って推移してい
る。12 月のスタッフ見通しでは物価予想がさらに上方修正される可能性が高い。 
ただし、エネルギー高が消費者物価を直接押し上げることだけを理由に、ＥＣＢが利上げを続
けるとは限らない。12 月の判断では、①原油・天然ガス価格の水準と持続性、②企業による価格
転嫁の動向、③賃金への二次的波及、④中長期の期待インフレ率、⑤長期金利上昇と銀行融資の
厳格化、⑥フランスを中心とする国債市場の混乱と他国への波及―などが重要となってこよう。 
筆者は、12 月に25bpの追加利上げを行い、預金ファシリティ金利を 2.75％に引き上げるとの見
通しを維持する。その後も 2027 年３月と６月に25bpずつ追加利上げを実施し、政策金利が
3.25％に達すると予想する。現時点で基調的なインフレや賃金への波及は限定的だが、エネルギ
ー高が長期化するなか、インフレ率が 2028 年まで２％目標を上回る可能性が高いためだ。 
一方、９月時点と比べて、追加利上げを取り巻く環境は複雑になっている。長期金利の上昇が
今後も続き、銀行融資や企業投資、家計消費への悪影響が強まれば、政策金利を引き上げる必要
性は低下する。また、フランスの財政不安が他国の国債市場に飛び火し、銀行システムに波及す
る場合には、ＥＣＢは物価安定と市場の安定を同時に考慮しなければならなくなる。反対に、イ
ラン情勢の緊張が続き、原油・天然ガス価格が一段と上昇すれば、企業の価格転嫁や賃金への波
及が強まり、従来の予想以上に金融引き締めが必要となる可能性もある。 
 インフレ圧力が強まる一方で、金融環境も引き締まるという難しい状況のなか、今回の理事会
では利上げを急がず、新たなデータを見極める姿勢を確認した。今後の金融政策は、エネルギー
価格の上昇による二次的波及と、長期金利上昇を通じた需要抑制効果のどちらが優勢になるかに
左右されることになろう。

以 上

## Document 537: タイGDP（26年7-9月期）～成長率は2.9％に加速、電子関連輸出と設備投資がけん引

Source PDF: all report pdf/タイGDP（26年7-9月期）～成長率は2.9％に加速、電子関連輸出と設備投資がけん引.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

【結果概要】 
タイの2026年7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率（速報値）1は前年同期比 2.9％となり、前期の同 2.4％
（改定値）から加速した（図表１）。Bloomberg調査の市場予想2（3.1％）を小幅に下回ったものの、
前年同期比では２四半期ぶりに伸びが高まった。また、季節調整済み前期比成長率は 0.3％となり、
前期のほぼ横ばい（0.0％）から小幅に加速した。 
需要項目別にみると、AI・データセンター関連の世界的な需要拡大を背景に輸出が加速し、その
伸び率は鈍化した輸入を上回った。民間投資も機械・設備投資を中心に一段と加速したほか、民間
消費は自動車購入の増加を受けて持ち直し、公共投資も増加に転じた。一方、政府消費や民間建設
投資は減少した。 
NESDCは2026年通年の成長率見通しを従来の2.0～2.5％から 2.5％へ上方修正した。また、2027
年の成長率は2.0～3.0％（中央値 2.5％）と予想した。

【支出別の詳細】

久財は 12.6％と前期（3.7％）から大幅に加速した。特に自動車購入が 20.9％と高い伸びを示した
ほか、非耐久財も 2.6％と小幅に加速した。一方、半耐久財は 3.0％、サービス消費は 0.8％といず

【景気の見方と今後の注目点】 
7-9月期のタイ経済は前年同期比2.9％と前期（2.4％）から加速し、季節調整済み前期比も 0.3％
と前期のほぼ横ばいから持ち直した。AI・データセンター関連の需要拡大を背景に電子機器の輸出
が伸び、機械・設備投資も好調だった。製造業や小売・卸売業が加速した一方、民間消費は自動車
購入がけん引し、サービス消費は鈍化した。宿泊・飲食業や建設業にも弱さが残っており、景気回
復の裾野はなお広がりを欠いている。 
先行きの焦点は、好調な輸出と民間投資が国内経済にどこまで波及するかである。NESDCは2026
年の成長率見通しを従来の 2.0～2.5％から 2.5％へ上方修正した。電子機器などへの需要拡大を背
景に、財輸出額（ドルベース）の伸びを 15.1％から 20.4％、民間投資を 9.6％から 14.1％へ引き
上げた一方、民間消費は 2.6％から 2.3％へ下方修正した。輸出や設備投資の拡大に伴い中間財・
資本財の輸入も増えており、こうした好調さが幅広い国内企業の雇用や所得の改善につながるかが
注目される。 
2027年は輸出・投資の増勢鈍化が見込まれる。NESDCは同年の成長率を2.0～3.0％（中央値2.5％）
と予想している。世界経済・貿易の減速などを背景に、財輸出額の伸びは2026年の20.4％から8.6％、
民間投資は 14.1％から 7.9％へ低下する見通しだ。輸出・民間投資の増勢鈍化が見込まれるなか、
公共投資や観光の回復、純輸出の改善が景気をどこまで下支えするかが注目される。 
リスク面では、米国の通商政策や中東情勢の不確実性に加え、世界的な AI関連投資の減速に注
意が必要である。AI関連需要が弱まれば、足元の成長を支える電子機器輸出や設備投資が下押しさ
れるおそれがある。また、エルニーニョに伴う干ばつは農業生産や農家所得を圧迫する可能性があ
り、高水準の家計債務も消費回復の重石となる。こうしたリスクが顕在化し、内需の回復が遅れれ
ば、景気の下押し圧力が強まる可能性がある。

本資料記載のデータは各種の情報源から入手・加工したものであり、その正確性と完全性を保証するものではありません。 
また、本資料は情報提供が目的であり、記載の意見や予測は、いかなる契約の締結や解約を勧誘するものではありません。

## Document 538: タイ・７-９月GDPは外需主導で底堅さをみせるも、先行きは不透明 ～AI・半導体需要が下支えする一方、インフレと異常気象が内需の重しに～

Source PDF: all report pdf/タイ・７-９月GDPは外需主導で底堅さをみせるも、先行きは不透明 ～AI・半導体需要が下支えする一方、インフレと異常気象が内需の重しに～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026年11月 16日

1/4 
タイ・７-９月 GDPは外需主導で底堅さをみせるも、先行きは不透明 
～AI・半導体需要が下支えする一方、インフレと異常気象が内需の重しに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ タイ経済では、イラン情勢の悪化に伴うエネルギー価格の上昇にバーツ安による輸入物価の
上昇が重なり、インフレ圧力が強まっている。10月のインフレ率は前年同月比＋3.90％と中
銀の目標レンジを上回り、2023年１月以来の高い伸びとなった。バーツ安の背景には、金価
格の調整に加え、原油高による対外収支の悪化や、家計支援策・洪水対策に伴う財政負担の
拡大など、経済のファンダメンタルズの脆弱化に対する懸念がある。 
○ 一方、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大を背景とする財輸出の堅調さや、サービス輸出
の拡大、データセンター関連投資の活発化が景気を下支えしている。７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成
長率は前年同期比＋2.9％と前期（同＋2.4％）から伸びが加速したものの、前期比では＋0.3％
（年率換算＋1.35％）にとどまり、景気の勢いは力強さを欠いている。個人消費も政府の補
助金政策に支えられるなど、自律的な景気拡大には至っていない。 
○ 政府は2026年の経済成長率を＋2.5％、2027年を＋2.0～3.0％と見込んでいる。先行きもＡ
Ｉ・半導体関連需要が景気を下支えすると期待される一方、インフレの高止まりや政策支援
効果の剥落、データセンター関連投資の鈍化が懸念される。さらに、エルニーニョ現象に伴
う高温・少雨が農業生産や製造業の活動を下押しするとともに、食料品や電力価格の上昇を
通じてインフレ圧力を強める可能性がある。外需の堅調さが景気を支える一方、生活必需品
を中心とするインフレが内需を圧迫し、低成長が長期化するリスクに注意が必要である。

### Page 2

2/4 
【エネルギー価格の上昇にバーツ安が重なり、インフレ圧力が一段と強まる展開】 
アジア新興国は、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う原油高をはじめとするエネルギー価格の上昇が景気の
重しとなることが懸念されている。タイ
では、2025年半ば以降、インフレ率がマ
イナス圏で推移するなど物価下落が続
いてきたものの、2026年４月以降は一転
してプラスに転じている。直近の 10月
のインフレ率は前年同月比＋3.90％と
中銀の目標レンジ（２±１％）を上回り、
2023 年１月以来の高い伸びとなってい
る（図１）。コアインフレ率は前年同月比
＋1.58％と目標レンジ内で推移してい
るものの、2023年４月以来の高い伸びと
なるなど、着実にインフレ圧力が強まる動きがみられる。 
インフレ圧力が広がりをみせている背
景には、金融市場において通貨バーツ相
場が下落基調を強めており、輸入物価の
上昇が幅広い財価格を押し上げているこ
とが影響している。タイでは伝統的に金
の取引が活発であり、バーツ相場は年明
けからしばらくの間、金の国際価格に連
動する形で強含んできた。しかし、足元
では金価格が調整の動きを強めており、
バーツ相場の重しとなっている可能性が
ある。さらに、原油高が輸入額を押し上
げて対外収支を悪化させるとともに、政府による家計支援を目的とする補助金政策が財政負担を膨
らませるなど、経済のファンダメンタルズ（基礎的条件）の脆弱化が警戒されている。そのうえ、
９月中旬以降に豪雨による洪水被害が広がったことを受けて、政府は洪水対策を目的とする財政支
援を拡充しており、公的債務残高は法定上限（ＧＤＰ比 70％）に近付いている（注１）。こうした
事情も、バーツ安の動きが強まる一因になってきたと考えられる。

注１ 10月２日付レポート「タイ、洪水被害が景気の新たな重石に」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666229.html）
